- **pi**: The PI (Principal Investigator) — the worker that produced a successful route or an accepted revision of it. Receives the three review summaries, modifies the route and rebuts in the 103–118 window, writes a change list; persists across rounds as the route's defender — the PI of the latest accepted version. A PI superseded by a newer accepted version closes after the handover. - **pi-gpu-sandbox-2026-11**: - **観測日**: 2026-11-11〜12 - **問い**: GPU を使う作業を、秘密を守りながら、pi の sandbox の中でできるか。どの方式が手軽か。 - **結論**: 調べた拡張のどれも、設定だけでは GPU を通せない。`@yuru7/pi-sandbox` の `runnerCommand` に小さなラッパーを差すと、GPU・docker の遮断・worktree の commit・hooks の保護が揃う。採用の判断は [ADR-0035](../../adr/0035-pi-sandbox-gpu-runner.md)、仕様は [pi-sandbox-runner](../../spec/sandbox/pi-sandbox-runner.md)。 ([applejxd/dotfiles](https://github.com/applejxd/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/docs/research/agents/pi-gpu-sandbox-2026-11.md))

([magnusminds/PIDA_Multi_Agent_Project_Intelligence_Starter_Kit](https://github.com/magnusminds/PIDA_Multi_Agent_Project_Intelligence_Starter_Kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pida-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **pida-reviewer**: Read-only PIDA reviewer that independently reviews the current diff against a task's acceptance criteria for correctness, regressions, security, test quality, compatibility, and documentation drift. Use from /project-review or before marking a task Done. Never edits files. ([magnusminds/PIDA_Multi_Agent_Project_Intelligence_Starter_Kit](https://github.com/magnusminds/PIDA_Multi_Agent_Project_Intelligence_Starter_Kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pida-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **pida-test-runner**: PIDA test runner that discovers the repository's real quality gates (tests, lint, type checks, builds) from project config and CI, runs the requested ones locally, and reports exact commands and outcomes. Use from /project-implement or /project-review to verify a change. Does not modify source or tests. ([magnusminds/PIDA_Multi_Agent_Project_Intelligence_Starter_Kit](https://github.com/magnusminds/PIDA_Multi_Agent_Project_Intelligence_Starter_Kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pida-test-runner.md) · ★1)
- **PIDE-DISTRIBUTION-AGENT-QUERY**: &gt; Consulta `docs/regulatory/pide_2017/INDEX.md` y la matriz de trazabilidad. Necesito el estudio técnico-económico de distribución especificando entidad, horizonte, año base, GIS, cargas nuevas, precio y tasa con fuentes verificadas. Distíngueme tres niveles: (A) norma vigente consolidada; (B) RM 428-2017-MEM/DM de prepublicación; (C) TDR Electro Puno 2018. Sin el texto del anexo MINEM, NO afirmes ([Mac-Tapia/power-intelligence](https://github.com/Mac-Tapia/power-intelligence/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/PIDE-DISTRIBUTION-AGENT-QUERY.md))
- **pidex-analyst**: Research and analysis specialist in the pidex-* pipeline for code-level investigation. Converts unknowns to knowns through active code execution and POCs, not theoretical hypotheses. Use proactively when pidex-planner or pidex-implementer encounters unknown APIs, unverified assumptions, or needs a proof-of-concept. After investigation, the next logical step is @agent-pidex-planner (if the analysis leads to planning) or back to the caller. ([d-trattner/pidex](https://github.com/d-trattner/pidex/blob/HEAD/agents/pidex-analyst.md) · ★2)
- **pidex-architect**: Architectural coherence specialist in the pidex-* pipeline. Owns `agents.output/architecture/system-architecture.md` as evergreen single source of truth. Reviews plans for architectural fit, maintains ADRs, audits technical debt. Use proactively when pidex-planner produces a plan that touches core architecture, when technical approach needs validation, or for periodic health audits. ([d-trattner/pidex](https://github.com/d-trattner/pidex/blob/HEAD/agents/pidex-architect.md) · ★2)
- **pidex-code-reviewer**: Reviews implemented code in the pidex-* pipeline for quality, maintainability, and architecture alignment BEFORE security review. Use proactively after @agent-pidex-implementer completes an implementation. Has authority to REJECT based on code quality. Produces APPROVED / APPROVED_WITH_COMMENTS / REJECTED verdict. After approval, the next logical step is @agent-pidex-security. ([d-trattner/pidex](https://github.com/d-trattner/pidex/blob/HEAD/agents/pidex-code-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **pidex-critic**: Program-manager-style reviewer in the pidex-* pipeline that stress-tests planning documents BEFORE implementation. Use proactively after @agent-pidex-planner produces a plan. Assesses value statement, architectural fit, scope, technical debt, unresolved questions. Produces a critique doc with APPROVED / APPROVED_WITH_COMMENTS / REJECTED verdict. After approval, the next logical step is @agent-pidex-implementer. ([d-trattner/pidex](https://github.com/d-trattner/pidex/blob/HEAD/agents/pidex-critic.md) · ★2)
- **pidex-designer**: Visual design authority in the pidex-* pipeline. Owns `agents.output/design/DESIGN.md` as evergreen design system doc. Reviews plans for UI/UX quality, maintains design tokens, audits for AI-slop patterns. Use after pidex-critic approves a plan that has UI/frontend components, before pidex-implementer starts coding. ([d-trattner/pidex](https://github.com/d-trattner/pidex/blob/HEAD/agents/pidex-designer.md) · ★2)
- **pidex-devops**: DevOps specialist in the pidex-* pipeline for packaging, versioning, deployment readiness, and release execution. Two-stage model — commit locally on plan approval, push/deploy only on user's explicit release approval. Use proactively after @agent-pidex-uat marks APPROVED FOR RELEASE. After release, the next logical step is @agent-pidex-retrospective. ([d-trattner/pidex](https://github.com/d-trattner/pidex/blob/HEAD/agents/pidex-devops.md) · ★2)
- **pidex-implementer**: Execution-focused coding agent in the pidex-* pipeline. Implements approved plans with strict TDD-first discipline — writes failing tests before implementation, minimal code to pass, refactors. Use proactively after @agent-pidex-critic approves a plan. After completion, the next logical step is @agent-pidex-code-reviewer for the quality gate. ([d-trattner/pidex](https://github.com/d-trattner/pidex/blob/HEAD/agents/pidex-implementer.md) · ★2)
- **pidex-pi**: Process Improvement specialist in the pidex-* pipeline. Analyzes retrospectives and proposes or implements explicitly approved workflow improvements. Never modifies application source. Producer-bound completion returns to the orchestrator. ([d-trattner/pidex](https://github.com/d-trattner/pidex/blob/HEAD/agents/pidex-pi.md) · ★2)
- **pidex-planner**: High-rigor planning assistant in the pidex-* pipeline. Translates roadmap epics into implementation-ready plans with acceptance criteria, milestones, and verification steps. Use proactively after @agent-pidex-roadmap defines an epic, or when the user wants to plan a specific feature. Produces WHAT/WHY, never HOW. After plan completion, the next logical step is @agent-pidex-critic for review. ([d-trattner/pidex](https://github.com/d-trattner/pidex/blob/HEAD/agents/pidex-planner.md) · ★2)
- **pidex-qa**: Test specialist in the pidex-* pipeline verifying test coverage and execution. Designs test strategies from user perspective, audits implementer tests skeptically, validates TDD compliance as a gate. Use proactively after @agent-pidex-code-reviewer approves implementation. After QA pass, the next logical step is @agent-pidex-uat. ([d-trattner/pidex](https://github.com/d-trattner/pidex/blob/HEAD/agents/pidex-qa.md) · ★2)
- **pidex-retrospective**: Captures lessons learned, process improvements, and architectural patterns after implementation in the pidex-* pipeline. Use proactively after @agent-pidex-devops completes a release, or after a UAT-complete plan cycle. Focus: repeatable process improvements, not one-off technical details. After retrospective, the next logical step is @agent-pidex-pi to extract and apply process changes. ([d-trattner/pidex](https://github.com/d-trattner/pidex/blob/HEAD/agents/pidex-retrospective.md) · ★2)
- **pidex-roadmap**: Product vision holder in the pidex-* pipeline. Defines outcome-focused epics, maps them to releases, maintains the master product roadmap. Use proactively when the user wants to define new features at the epic level, update strategic direction, track release status, or answer "what should we build and why". NOT for implementation details. After defining an epic, the next logical step is @agent-pidex-planner for breakdown. ([d-trattner/pidex](https://github.com/d-trattner/pidex/blob/HEAD/agents/pidex-roadmap.md) · ★2)
- **pidex-security**: Comprehensive security audit specialist in the pidex-* pipeline. Covers architectural security, code security, dependency security, and compliance. Use proactively when pidex-planner produces a plan touching auth/payments/sensitive data, when pidex-implementer completes code needing security review, or for pre-production gates. Has a mandatory clarification gate — will ask which mode (Full Audit / Targeted / Dependency-Only / Pre-Production Gate) before starting. ([d-trattner/pidex](https://github.com/d-trattner/pidex/blob/HEAD/agents/pidex-security.md) · ★2)
- **pidex-uat**: Product Owner conducting UAT in the pidex-* pipeline to verify implementation delivers stated business value. Document-based review, not code inspection — relies on Implementation, Code Review, and QA docs as evidence. Use proactively after @agent-pidex-qa marks QA Complete. Fast process when docs are present. After approval, the next logical step is @agent-pidex-devops. ([d-trattner/pidex](https://github.com/d-trattner/pidex/blob/HEAD/agents/pidex-uat.md) · ★2)
- **pidex-wiki-hygienist**: Read-only wiki hygiene auditor and conservative cleanup planner for PIDEX project wikis.
